## Scope and selection

This analysis uses the four WANDS full-results Parquet exports for `agentic_gpt6_luna_bm25_e5` from run batch `20261024T210030Z_34814`, seeds 43, 44, 45, and 46. Each run covers the same 480 queries. A query is selected when its `NDCG_G` is below 0.25 in all four seeds. The `drudge report` query (ID 366) also scores zero in all four runs, but is excluded because its judged ideal DCG (`idcg_g`) is zero. The overall table reports all four seed scores and their mean. Per-query rankings show the ideal positive-grade WANDS judgments beside the seed-43 agentic results. Product images are 72×72-pixel lazy-loaded thumbnails. Hover over a product title to see its full catalog description; blank source descriptions are identified in the tooltip. Ideal results are ordered by grade descending, then document ID ascending, up to 10; they are not padded with grade-0 documents. `NA` means the agentic result has no WANDS judgment; grade 0 is reserved for documents explicitly judged irrelevant.

option punch-out drainage holes complete the design . inside a self-watering planter , the potting mix is held above the waterline by a sturdy screen . the water chamber is connected by a small porous
